Style status: Sesuai gaya

Create ONE square GELITA avatar. Use the attached woven bamboo object as SHAPE reference for a bundengan from Wonosobo: a broad curved oval duck-herder bamboo hood (kowangan), open concave face visible in three-quarter view, woven basket canopy curling back, short bamboo lattice base, FOUR thin strings spanning INSIDE its open hollow canopy. It must read as a protective woven hood converted into a string instrument, NOT a guitar, lute, drum, gourd or long-necked instrument. Render this motif as one large sculptural GOLD emblem centered on a warm cream parchment disc. Surround it with the SAME family of thick dimensional dark-navy circular medallion rim, thin brass edging and four small gold diamond points at cardinal directions used by the other GELITA avatars. Premium stylized 3D animated adventure game; softly rounded dimensional materials, warm gold highlights, dark navy and cream, readable at 48 pixels. No human face, no person, no lettering, no watermark. Whole medallion visible with padding. Genuinely transparent exterior.
Delivery: 512 × 512 WebP; preserve transparent margins.

Negative: No watermarks, signatures, unintended text, extra limbs, duplicate subjects, cropped key features, blurry edges, neon colours, clutter, or fake checkerboard transparency. No painterly 2D, gouache, paper grain, or conflicting Jaka costume.
